  148 | if (appliesTo("Storage Capacity", searchTerm)) test(`${searchTerm}: verifyingStorageCapacityFilterFunctionality `, async ({ page }, testInfo) =>{

  153 |

  179 | 		// 2. Data extracted: drop products whose page details were not read (not a filter defect).

  186 |

  199 | 			if (match.status === "unknown") {
  200 | 				console.warn(`[${testName}] No usable Storage Capacity row for filter '${filter}' on product '${title}'; not counting it as a mismatch`);
  201 | 				skipped++;
  202 | 				continue;

  204 | 			compared++;
  205 | 
  206 | 			if (match.status === "mismatch") {

  227 | // Battery: Amazon offers different battery filters per product type (phones: Battery Capacity and Battery Life, tablets: Battery Power
  228 | // and Battery Life, laptops and speakers: Battery Life, headphones: Battery Charge Time). So one test per search term looks at which of
  229 | // them the left navigation shows and checks each one it finds.
  230 | // `rows` are the spec-table rows read for that filter (Amazon calls the same thing "Battery Life" or "Battery Average Life").
  231 | const batteryFilters = [
  232 |   { heading: "Battery Life", key: "batterylife", rows: ["battery life", "battery average life"], options: (p: ProductListingPage): Locator => p.listBatteryLifeOptionsBy },
  233 |   { heading: "Battery Capacity", key: "batterycapacity", rows: ["battery capacity"], options: (p: ProductListingPage): Locator => p.listBatteryCapacityOptionsBy },
  234 |   { heading: "Battery Power", key: "batterypower", rows: ["battery power"], options: (p: ProductListingPage): Locator => p.listBatteryPowerOptionsBy },
  235 |   { heading: "Battery Charge Time", key: "batterychargetime", rows: ["battery charge time"], options: (p: ProductListingPage): Locator => p.listBatteryChargeTimeOptionsBy },
  236 | ];
  237 | 
  238 | if (appliesTo("Battery", searchTerm)) test(`${searchTerm}: verifyingBatteryFiltersFunctionality `, async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
  239 | 
  240 | 	await page.addInitScript(() => {
  241 | 		Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'webdriver', { get: () => false });
  242 | 	});
  243 | 
  244 | 	const landingPage = new AmazonLandingPage(page);
  245 | 	const productPage = new ProductListingPage(page);
  246 | 	const genericUtility = new GenericUtility(page,testInfo);
  247 | 	const sharedFilterFlows = new SharedFilterFlows(page,testInfo);
  248 | 	const testName: string = testInfo.title;
  249 | 
  250 | 	await landingPage.openingLandingPage();
  251 | 	await genericUtility.clearBlockers();
  252 | 	await landingPage.givingInputWithinSearchBar(searchTerm);
  253 | 	await landingPage.clickingOnSubmitSearchButton();
  254 | 
  255 | 	// Which of the battery filters does this page show? The test is skipped if it shows none of them.
  256 | 	await genericUtility.clearBlockers();
  257 | 	const offered: string[] = await genericUtility.requireAnyFilter(batteryFilters.map((battery) => battery.heading));
  258 | 	console.log(`[${testName}] Battery filters shown for '${searchTerm}': ${offered.join(', ')}`);
  259 | 
  260 | 	for (const battery of batteryFilters.filter((b) => offered.some((name) => name.toLowerCase() === b.heading.toLowerCase()))) {
  261 | 		console.log(`[${testName}] ===== ${battery.heading} =====`);
  262 | 		sharedFilterFlows.notAppliedOptions = [];
  263 | 
  264 | 		let results:Map<string,string | number |boolean |null |string[]>[] =await sharedFilterFlows.applyFilterAndValidateProductsWithResult(battery.options(productPage),battery.key);
  265 | 
  266 | 		// 1. Filter applied: every option should have been clicked and shown as applied.
  267 | 		expect.soft(sharedFilterFlows.notAppliedOptions.length, `${battery.heading}: every filter option was applied (not applied: ${sharedFilterFlows.notAppliedOptions.join(', ') || 'none'})`).toBe(0);
  268 | 		// 2. Data extracted: drop products whose page details were not read (not a filter defect).
  269 | 		results = genericUtility.onlyCapturedProducts(results, testName);
  270 | 		// 3. List has data: nothing validated means nothing was checked, so this filter fails (the other filters still run).
  271 | 		expect.soft(results.length, `${battery.heading}: at least one product was validated (the filter returned products and a product page could be read)`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  272 | 
  273 | 		console.log(`[${testName}] ${battery.heading}: retrieved ${results.length} results from applyFilterAndValidateProductsWithResult.`);
  274 | 
  275 | 		let compared:number = 0;
  276 | 		let skipped:number = 0;
  277 | 		let mismatched:number = 0;
  278 | 		for (const product of results) {
  279 | 			const filter:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("filter"));
  280 | 			const title:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("title"));
  281 | 			const keyFeatures:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("keyFeatures"));
  282 | 			const about :string= genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("about"));
  283 | 			const techDetails:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("techDetails"));
  284 | 			const match = rangeMatch(filter, battery.rows, { title, keyFeatures, about, techDetails });
  285 | 
  286 | 			// 4. Comparable: without a matching row in the spec table nothing can be said, so the product is skipped.
  287 | 			if (match.status === "unknown") {
  288 | 				console.warn(`[${testName}] No usable ${battery.heading} row for filter '${filter}' on product '${title}'; not counting it as a mismatch`);
  289 | 				skipped++;
  290 | 				continue;
  291 | 			}
  292 | 			compared++;
  293 | 
  294 | 			if (match.status === "mismatch") {
  295 | 				mismatched++;
  296 | 				console.error(`[${testName}] Mismatch found for ${battery.heading} filter '${filter}' on product '${title}' (row says: ${match.rowValue})`);
  297 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] Key Features: ${keyFeatures}`);
  298 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] About: ${about}`);
  299 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] Tech Details: ${techDetails}`);
  300 | 
  301 | 				expect.soft(match.status, `${battery.heading}: filter '${filter}' does not fit the product (row says: ${match.rowValue}): ${title.slice(0, 80)}`).toBe("match");
  302 | 			} else {
  303 | 				console.log(`[${testName}] ${battery.heading} filter '${filter}' fits the row (${match.rowValue}) on '${title.slice(0, 60)}'`);
  304 | 			}
  305 | 		}
  306 | 		console.log(`[${testName}] ${battery.heading} summary: ${results.length} products read, ${compared} compared, ${skipped} skipped (nothing to compare), ${mismatched} did not fit the filter.`);
  307 | 		// 5. Something compared: if no product could be compared, nothing was checked for this filter.
  308 | 		expect.soft(compared, `${battery.heading}: at least one product could be compared with the filter`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  309 | 	}
  310 | 
  311 | });
